{"parentUuid":"52f91d0c-dbee-492e-a25f-791f56780bfc","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a3d5d65c592ec9f5d","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DKLYo8HXuL7BBvmuXeenSQ","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Hook for saving and loading design compositions to/from Supabase.\n     3→ */\n     4→\n     5→import { useCallback, useState } from 'react'\n     6→\n     7→interface SaveCompositionParams {\n     8→  fabricJson: object\n     9→  previewDataUrl: string\n    10→  productType: string\n    11→  productId: string\n    12→  compositionId?: string\n    13→}\n    14→\n    15→interface SaveResult {\n    16→  composition_id: string\n    17→  preview_url: string\n    18→}\n    19→\n    20→interface LoadResult {\n    21→  id: string\n    22→  schema_version: number\n    23→  layers: object\n    24→  product_type: string\n    25→  product_id: string\n    26→  preview_url: string\n    27→  status: string\n    28→}\n    29→\n    30→export function useDesignPersistence() {\n    31→  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false)\n    32→  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false)\n    33→\n    34→  const save = useCallback(async (params: SaveCompositionParams): Promise<SaveResult | null> => {\n    35→    setIsSaving(true)\n    36→    try {\n    37→      const res = await fetch('/api/designs/compose-v2', {\n    38→        method: 'POST',\n    39→        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    40→        body: JSON.stringify(params),\n    41→      })\n    42→\n    43→      if (!res.ok) {\n    44→        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({ error: 'Save failed' }))\n    45→        throw new Error(err.error || 'Save failed')\n    46→      }\n    47→\n    48→      return await res.json()\n    49→    } catch (error) {\n    50→      console.error('Failed to save composition:', error)\n    51→      return null\n    52→    } finally {\n    53→      setIsSaving(false)\n    54→    }\n    55→  }, [])\n    56→\n    57→  const load = useCallback(async (compositionId: string): Promise<LoadResult | null> => {\n    58→    setIsLoading(true)\n    59→    try {\n    60→      const res = await fetch(`/api/designs/composition/${compositionId}`)\n    61→      if (!res.ok) return null\n    62→      return await res.json()\n    63→    } catch {\n    64→      return null\n    65→    } finally {\n    66→      setIsLoading(false)\n    67→    }\n    68→  }, [])\n    69→\n    70→  return { save, load, isSaving, isLoading }\n    71→}\n    72→"}]},"uuid":"44eb96f6-40ad-432c-8d82-f1e5b10d22b8","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:18.649Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"52f91d0c-dbee-492e-a25f-791f56780bfc"}

    1→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     2→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n     3→import { createServerClient } from '@/lib/supabase-server'\n     4→\n     5→export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n     6→  try {\n     7→    // Authenticate user\n     8→    const { user } = await createServerClient(request)\n     9→    if (!user) {\n    10→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    11→    }\n    12→\n    13→    const body = await request.json()\n    14→    const { fabricJson, previewDataUrl, productType, productId, compositionId } = body\n    15→\n    16→    if (!fabricJson || !productId) {\n    17→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing required fields' }, { status: 400 })\n    18→    }\n    19→\n    20→    const id = compositionId || crypto.randomUUID()\n    21→\n    22→    // If updating existing composition, verify ownership\n    23→    if (compositionId) {\n    24→      const { data: existing } = await supabaseAdmin\n    25→        .from('design_compositions')\n    26→        .select('user_id')\n    27→        .eq('id', compositionId)\n    28→        .single()\n    29→      if (existing && existing.user_id !== user.id) {\n    30→        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden' }, { status: 403 })\n    31→      }\n    32→    }\n    33→\n    34→    // Upload preview image to Supabase Storage\n    35→    let previewUrl: string | null = null\n    36→    if (previewDataUrl && previewDataUrl.startsWith('data:image/png;base64,')) {\n    37→      const base64Data = previewDataUrl.replace(/^data:image\\/png;base64,/, '')\n    38→      const buffer = Buffer.from(base64Data, 'base64')\n    39→\n    40→      const filename = `compositions/${id}/preview.png`\n    41→      const { error: uploadError } = await supabaseAdmin.storage\n    42→        .from('designs')\n    43→        .upload(filename, buffer, {\n    44→          contentType: 'image/png',\n    45→          cacheControl: '31536000',\n    46→          upsert: true,\n    47→        })\n    48→\n    49→      if (!uploadError) {\n    50→        const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabaseAdmin.storage\n    51→          .from('designs')\n    52→          .getPublicUrl(filename)\n    53→        previewUrl = publicUrl\n    54→      }\n    55→    }\n    56→\n    57→    // Upsert composition record\n    58→    const { error: dbError } = await supabaseAdmin\n    59→      .from('design_compositions')\n    60→      .upsert({\n    61→        id,\n    62→        user_id: user.id,\n    63→        schema_version: 2,\n    64→        layers: fabricJson,\n    65→        product_type: productType || 'tshirt',\n    66→        product_id: productId,\n    67→        preview_url: previewUrl,\n    68→        status: 'draft',\n    69→        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    70→      }, { onConflict: 'id' })\n    71→\n    72→    if (dbError) {\n    73→      console.error('Failed to save composition:', dbError)\n    74→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Database error' }, { status: 500 })\n    75→    }\n    76→\n    77→    return NextResponse.json({\n    78→      success: true,\n    79→      composition_id: id,\n    80→      preview_url: previewUrl,\n    81→    })\n    82→  } catch (error) {\n    83→    console.error('compose-v2 error:', error)\n    84→    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Internal server error' }, { status: 500 })\n    85→  }\n    86→}\n    87→"}]},"uuid":"3a56d339-1955-42fd-b2ba-63e6f69bf0f7","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:21.079Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f005412e-d66a-4c06-b8eb-794765964e4f"}

{"parentUuid":"2867a673-b885-4558-b107-cc45ed8f8a03","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a3d5d65c592ec9f5d","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HqbJrU3j6DR852DgrMcTpN","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Design Composition API\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/designs/compose\n     5→ * Creates a design composition from multiple layers, generates a preview,\n     6→ * and stores the composition in the database.\n     7→ */\n     8→\n     9→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n    10→import { cookies } from 'next/headers'\n    11→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n    12→import { renderCompositionPreview, type CompositionLayer } from '@/lib/composition-renderer'\n    13→\n    14→export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n    15→  try {\n    16→    // ── Auth ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    17→    const cookieStore = await cookies()\n    18→    const token = cookieStore.get('sb-access-token')?.value\n    19→    if (!token) {\n    20→      return NextResponse.json(\n    21→        { error: 'Authentication required' },\n    22→        { status: 401 }\n    23→      )\n    24→    }\n    25→\n    26→    const { data: { user } } = await supabaseAdmin.auth.getUser(token)\n    27→    if (!user) {\n    28→      return NextResponse.json(\n    29→        { error: 'Authentication required' },\n    30→        { status: 401 }\n    31→      )\n    32→    }\n    33→\n    34→    // ── Parse body ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    35→    const body = await request.json()\n    36→    const {\n    37→      layers,\n    38→      product_type,\n    39→      product_id,\n    40→    }: {\n    41→      layers: CompositionLayer[]\n    42→      product_type: string\n    43→      product_id?: string\n    44→    } = body\n    45→\n    46→    // ── Validate ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    47→    if (!layers || !Array.isArray(layers) || layers.length === 0) {\n    48→      return NextResponse.json(\n    49→        { error: 'At least one layer is required' },\n    50→        { status: 400 }\n    51→      )\n    52→    }\n    53→\n    54→    if (!product_type || typeof product_type !== 'string') {\n    55→      return NextResponse.json(\n    56→        { error: 'product_type is required' },\n    57→        { status: 400 }\n    58→      )\n    59→    }\n    60→\n    61→    // Validate layer structure\n    62→    for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i++) {\n    63→      const layer = layers[i]\n    64→      if (!layer.type || !['text', 'image', 'ai'].includes(layer.type)) {\n    65→        return NextResponse.json(\n    66→          { error: `Invalid layer type at index ${i}: must be 'text', 'image', or 'ai'` },\n    67→          { status: 400 }\n    68→        )\n    69→      }\n    70→      if ((layer.type === 'image' || layer.type === 'ai') && !layer.url) {\n    71→        return NextResponse.json(\n    72→          { error: `Layer ${i} (${layer.type}) requires a url` },\n    73→          { status: 400 }\n    74→        )\n    75→      }\n    76→      if (layer.type === 'text' && !layer.text) {\n    77→        return NextResponse.json(\n    78→          { error: `Layer ${i} (text) requires text content` },\n    79→          { status: 400 }\n    80→        )\n    81→      }\n    82→    }\n    83→\n    84→    // Validate product_id exists if provided\n    85→    if (product_id) {\n    86→      const { data: product } = await supabaseAdmin\n    87→        .from('products')\n    88→        .select('id')\n    89→        .eq('id', product_id)\n    90→        .is('deleted_at', null)\n    91→        .single()\n    92→\n    93→      if (!product) {\n    94→        return NextResponse.json(\n    95→          { error: 'Product not found' },\n    96→          { status: 404 }\n    97→        )\n    98→      }\n    99→    }\n   100→\n   101→    // ── Render preview ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n   102→    const previewBuffer = await renderCompositionPreview(layers, product_type)\n   103→\n   104→    // ── Upload preview to Supabase Storage ──────────────────────────\n   105→    const compositionId = crypto.randomUUID()\n   106→    const filename = `compositions/${compositionId}/preview.png`\n   107→    let previewUrl: string\n   108→\n   109→    const { error: uploadError } = await supabaseAdmin.storage\n   110→      .from('designs')\n   111→      .upload(filename, previewBuffer, {\n   112→        contentType: 'image/png',\n   113→        cacheControl: '3600',\n   114→      })\n   115→\n   116→    if (uploadError) {\n   117→      console.error('Preview upload error:', uploadError)\n   118→      // Fallback to base64 data URL\n   119→      const base64 = previewBuffer.toString('base64')\n   120→      previewUrl = `data:image/png;base64,${base64}`\n   121→    } else {\n   122→      const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabaseAdmin.storage\n   123→        .from('designs')\n   124→        .getPublicUrl(filename)\n   125→      previewUrl = publicUrl\n   126→    }\n   127→\n   128→    // ── Create DB row ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n   129→    const { data: composition, error: insertError } = await supabaseAdmin\n   130→      .from('design_compositions')\n   131→      .insert({\n   132→        id: compositionId,\n   133→        user_id: user.id,\n   134→        product_type,\n   135→        product_id: product_id || null,\n   136→        schema_version: 1,\n   137→        layers,\n   138→        preview_url: previewUrl,\n   139→        status: 'draft',\n   140→      })\n   141→      .select('id, preview_url, status, created_at')\n   142→      .single()\n   143→\n   144→    if (insertError) {\n   145→      console.error('Composition insert error:', insertError)\n   146→      return NextResponse.json(\n   147→        { error: 'Failed to save composition', message: insertError.message },\n   148→        { status: 500 }\n   149→      )\n   150→    }\n   151→\n   152→    return NextResponse.json({\n   153→      success: true,\n   154→      composition_id: composition.id,\n   155→      preview_url: composition.preview_url,\n   156→    })\n   157→  } catch (error) {\n   158→    console.error('Designs compose API error:', error)\n   159→    return NextResponse.json(\n   160→      { error: 'Internal server error' },\n   161→      { status: 500 }\n   162→    )\n   163→  }\n   164→}\n   165→"}]},"uuid":"53ca54e6-aa58-41a3-aa31-1e5329e5234b","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:21.597Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2867a673-b885-4558-b107-cc45ed8f8a03"}

    1→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     2→import { cookies } from 'next/headers'\n     3→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n     4→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n     5→import { STORE_DEFAULTS } from '@/lib/store-config'\n     6→\n     7→const MAX_CART_QUANTITY = STORE_DEFAULTS.maxCartQuantity\n     8→\n     9→// GET /api/cart - Fetch cart items\n    10→export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {\n    11→  try {\n    12→    const cookieStore = await cookies()\n    13→    const sessionCookie = cookieStore.get('sb-access-token')\n    14→\n    15→    // Get session ID for guest carts (fallback)\n    16→    let sessionId = cookieStore.get('cart-session-id')?.value\n    17→    let needsSessionCookie = false\n    18→    if (!sessionId) {\n    19→      sessionId = crypto.randomUUID()\n    20→      needsSessionCookie = true\n    21→    }\n    22→\n    23→    const supabase = supabaseAdmin\n    24→\n    25→    // Try to get user from session\n    26→    let userId: string | null = null\n    27→    let userScopedClient: ReturnType<typeof createClient> | null = null\n    28→    if (sessionCookie) {\n    29→      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser(sessionCookie.value)\n    30→      userId = user?.id || null\n    31→\n    32→      // Create user-scoped client for RLS-protected queries (personalizations)\n    33→      if (userId) {\n    34→        userScopedClient = createClient(\n    35→          process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    36→          process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,\n    37→          {\n    38→            auth: { persistSession: false },\n    39→            global: { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${sessionCookie.value}` } },\n    40→          }\n    41→        )\n    42→      }\n    43→    }\n    44→\n    45→    // Fetch cart items (either by user_id or session_id)\n    46→    const query = supabase\n    47→      .from('cart_items')\n    48→      .select('id, product_id, quantity, variant_id, personalization_id, composition_id, created_at, updated_at')\n    49→      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })\n    50→\n    51→    if (userId) {\n    52→      query.eq('user_id', userId)\n    53→    } else {\n    54→      query.eq('session_id', sessionId)\n    55→    }\n    56→\n    57→    const { data: cartItems, error } = await query\n    58→\n    59→    if (error) {\n    60→      console.error('Cart fetch error:', error)\n    61→      return NextResponse.json(\n    62→        { error: 'Failed to fetch cart', message: error.message },\n    63→        { status: 500 }\n    64→      )\n    65→    }\n    66→\n    67→    // Fetch product details for each cart item\n    68→    // We need to get product_id list and query products + products_l10n\n    69→    const productIds = (cartItems || []).map((item: any) => item.product_id)\n    70→\n    71→    if (productIds.length === 0) {\n    72→      const response = NextResponse.json({ items: [] })\n    73→      if (needsSessionCookie) {\n    74→        response.cookies.set('cart-session-id', sessionId, {\n    75→          httpOnly: true,\n    76→          secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',\n    77→          sameSite: 'lax',\n    78→          maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30, // 30 days\n    79→        })\n    80→      }\n    81→      return response\n    82→    }\n    83→\n    84→    // Fetch products with their base info (include status for availability check)\n    85→    const { data: products, error: productsError } = await supabase\n    86→      .from('products')\n    87→      .select('id, title, base_price_cents, currency, images, status')\n    88→      .is('deleted_at', null)\n    89→      .in('id', productIds)\n    90→\n    91→    if (productsError) {\n    92→      console.error('Products fetch error:', productsError)\n    93→      return NextResponse.json(\n    94→        { error: 'Failed to fetch products', message: productsError.message },\n    95→        { status: 500 }\n    96→      )\n    97→    }\n    98→\n    99→    // Create a map of product details\n   100→    const productMap = new Map(\n   101→      (products || []).map((p: any) => {\n   102→        const img = Array.isArray(p.images) && p.images.length > 0\n   103→          ? (p.images[0].src || p.images[0].url || '')\n   104→          : ''\n   105→        return [\n   106→          p.id,\n   107→          {\n   108→            title: p.title || 'Unknown Product',\n   109→            price: p.base_price_cents ? p.base_price_cents / 100 : 0,\n   110→            image: img,\n   111→            currency: p.currency?.toUpperCase() || 'EUR',\n   112→            unavailable: p.status !== 'active',\n   113→          },\n   114→        ]\n   115→      })\n   116→    )\n   117→\n   118→    // Fetch variant details for cart items that have a variant_id\n   119→    const variantIds = (cartItems || [])\n   120→      .map((item: any) => item.variant_id)\n   121→      .filter(Boolean)\n   122→\n   123→    let variantMap = new Map<string, { size?: string; color?: string; price_cents?: number }>()\n   124→    if (variantIds.length > 0) {\n   125→      const { data: variants } = await supabase\n   126→        .from('product_variants')\n   127→        .select('id, size, color, price_cents')\n   128→        .in('id', variantIds)\n   129→\n   130→      variantMap = new Map(\n   131→        (variants || []).map((v: any) => [v.id, { size: v.size, color: v.color, price_cents: v.price_cents }])\n   132→      )\n   133→    }\n   134→\n   135→    // Fetch personalization details for cart items that have a personalization_id\n   136→    // Only authenticated users can have personalizations (RLS-protected)\n   137→    const personalizationIds = (cartItems || [])\n   138→      .map((item: any) => item.personalization_id)\n   139→      .filter(Boolean)\n   140→\n   141→    let personalizationMap = new Map<string, any>()\n   142→    if (personalizationIds.length > 0 && userScopedClient) {\n   143→      // Use user-scoped client to respect RLS policies on personalizations table\n   144→      const { data: personalizations } = await userScopedClient\n   145→        .from('personalizations')\n   146→        .select('id, text_content, font_family, font_color, font_size, position, preview_url')\n   147→        .in('id', personalizationIds)\n   148→\n   149→      personalizationMap = new Map(\n   150→        (personalizations || []).map((p: any) => [\n   151→          p.id,\n   152→          {\n   153→            text: p.text_content || '',\n   154→            font: p.font_family || 'Inter',\n   155→            fontColor: p.font_color || 160→        ])\n   161→      )\n   162→    }\n   163→\n   164→    // Fetch available variants for each product in the cart (for variant editing)\n   165→    const uniqueProductIds = [...new Set(productIds)]\n   166→    const availableVariants: Record<string, { sizes: string[]; colors: string[] }> = {}\n   167→\n   168→    if (uniqueProductIds.length > 0) {\n   169→      const { data: allVariants } = await supabase\n   170→        .from('product_variants')\n   171→        .select('product_id, size, color')\n   172→        .in('product_id', uniqueProductIds)\n   173→        .eq('is_enabled', true)\n   174→        .eq('is_available', true)\n   175→\n   176→      for (const pid of uniqueProductIds) {\n   177→        const productVariants = (allVariants || []).filter((v: any) => v.product_id === pid)\n   178→        const sizes = [...new Set(productVariants.map((v: any) => v.size).filter(Boolean))] as string[]\n   179→        const colors = [...new Set(productVariants.map((v: any) => v.color).filter(Boolean))] as string[]\n   180→        if (sizes.length > 0 || colors.length > 0) {\n   181→          availableVariants[pid] = { sizes, colors }\n   182→        }\n   183→      }\n   184→    }\n   185→\n   186→    // Transform cart items to include product details\n   187→    const items = (cartItems || []).map((item: any) => {\n   188→      const productDetails = productMap.get(item.product_id) || {\n   189→        title: 'Unknown Product',\n   190→        price: 0,\n   191→        image: '',\n   192→        currency: 'EUR',\n   193→        unavailable: true,\n   194→      }\n   195→\n   196→      return {\n   197→        id: item.id,\n   198→        product_id: item.product_id,\n   199→        variant_id: item.variant_id,\n   200→        quantity: item.quantity,\n   201→        product_title: productDetails.title,\n   202→        product_price: (() => {\n   203→          if (item.variant_id) {\n   204→            const vd = variantMap.get(item.variant_id)\n   205→            if (vd?.price_cents) return vd.price_cents / 100\n   206→          }\n   207→          return productDetails.price\n   208→        })(),\n   209→        product_image: productDetails.image || '',\n   210→        product_currency: productDetails.currency || 'EUR',\n   211→        unavailable: productDetails.unavailable || false,\n   212→        variant_details: item.variant_id ? (variantMap.get(item.variant_id) || {}) : {},\n   213→        personalization_id: item.personalization_id,\n   214→        composition_id: item.composition_id,\n   215→        personalization: item.personalization_id\n   216→          ? personalizationMap.get(item.personalization_id)\n   217→          : undefined,\n   218→      }\n   219→    })\n   220→\n   221→    const response = NextResponse.json({ items, available_variants: availableVariants })\n   222→    if (needsSessionCookie) {\n   223→      response.cookies.set('cart-session-id', sessionId, {\n   224→        httpOnly: true,\n   225→        secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',\n   226→        sameSite: 'lax',\n   227→        maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30, // 30 days\n   228→      })\n   229→    }\n   230→    return response\n   231→  } catch (error) {\n   232→    console.error('Cart API error:', error)\n   233→    return NextResponse.json(\n   234→      { error: 'Internal server error' },\n   235→      { status: 500 }\n   236→    )\n   237→  }\n   238→}\n   239→\n   240→// POST /api/cart - Add item to cart\n   241→export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n   242→  try {\n   243→    const cookieStore = await cookies()\n   244→    const sessionCookie = cookieStore.get('sb-access-token')\n   245→\n   246→    // Get session ID for guest carts\n   247→    let sessionId = cookieStore.get('cart-session-id')?.value\n   248→    let needsSessionCookie = false\n   249→    if (!sessionId) {\n   250→      sessionId = crypto.randomUUID()\n   251→      needsSessionCookie = true\n   252→    }\n   253→\n   254→    const supabase = supabaseAdmin\n   255→\n   256→    // Try to get user from session\n   257→    let userId: string | null = null\n   258→    if (sessionCookie) {\n   259→      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser(sessionCookie.value)\n   260→      userId = user?.id || null\n   261→    }\n   262→\n   263→    const body = await request.json()\n   264→    const { product_id, quantity, variant_details, personalization_id, composition_id } = body\n   265→\n   266→    if (!product_id || !quantity || quantity < 1) {\n   267→      return NextResponse.json(\n   268→        { error: 'Invalid request', message: 'product_id and quantity are required' },\n   269→        { status: 400 }\n   270→      )\n   271→    }\n   272→\n   273→    // Validate product exists and is active\n   274→    const { data: product } = await supabase\n   275→      .from('products')\n   276→      .select('id, status')\n   277→      .eq('id', product_id)\n   278→      .eq('status', 'active')\n   279→      .is('deleted_at', null)\n   280→      .single()\n   281→\n   282→    if (!product) {\n   283→      return NextResponse.json(\n   284→        { error: 'Product not found or unavailable', code: 'PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND' },\n   285→        { status: 404 }\n   286→      )\n   287→    }\n   288→\n   289→    // Resolve variant_id from variant_details (size/color)\n   290→    let variantId: string | null = null\n   291→    if (variant_details && (variant_details.size || variant_details.color)) {\n   292→      let variantQuery = supabase\n   293→        .from('product_variants')\n   294→        .select('id')\n   295→        .eq('product_id', product_id)\n   296→        .eq('is_enabled', true)\n   297→        .eq('is_available', true)\n   298→\n   299→      if (variant_details.size) {\n   300→        variantQuery = variantQuery.eq('size', variant_details.size)\n   301→      }\n   302→      if (variant_details.color) {\n   303→        variantQuery = variantQuery.eq('color', variant_details.color)\n   304→      }\n   305→\n   306→      const { data: matchedVariants } = await variantQuery.limit(1)\n   307→      if (matchedVariants && matchedVariants.length > 0) {\n   308→        variantId = matchedVariants[0].id\n   309→      }\n   310→    }\n   311→\n   312→    // If variant not resolved, try autoselect or reject\n   313→    if (!variantId) {\n   314→      const { data: availableVariants } = await supabase\n   315→        .from('product_variants')\n   316→        .select('id, size, color')\n   317→        .eq('product_id', product_id)\n   318→        .eq('is_enabled', true)\n   319→        .eq('is_available', true)\n   320→\n   321→      if (availableVariants && availableVariants.length === 1) {\n   322→        variantId = availableVariants[0].id\n   323→      } else if (availableVariants && availableVariants.length > 1) {\n   324→        return NextResponse.json(\n   325→          {\n   326→            error: 'Variant selection required',\n   327→            code: 'VARIANT_REQUIRED',\n   328→            message: 'This product requires selecting a size/color.',\n   329→            available_variants: availableVariants,\n   330→          },\n   331→          { status: 400 }\n   332→        )\n   333→      } else {\n   334→        return NextResponse.json(\n   335→          { error: 'No available variants for this product', code: 'NO_VARIANTS' },\n   336→          { status: 400 }\n   337→        )\n   338→      }\n   339→    }\n   340→\n   341→    // Check if item already exists in cart (same product + same variant + same personalization)\n   342→    // variantId is guaranteed non-null at this point\n   343→    const existingQuery = supabase\n   344→      .from('cart_items')\n   345→      .select('*')\n   346→      .eq('product_id', product_id)\n   347→      .eq('variant_id', variantId)\n   348→\n   349→    if 361→    if (userId) {\n   362→      existingQuery.eq('user_id', userId)\n   363→    } else {\n   364→      existingQuery.eq('session_id', sessionId)\n   365→    }\n   366→\n   367→    const { data: existingItems } = await existingQuery\n   368→\n   369→    if (existingItems && existingItems.length > 0) {\n   370→      // Update quantity if item already exists\n   371→      const existingItem = existingItems[0]\n   372→      const newQuantity = Math.min(existingItem.quantity + quantity, MAX_CART_QUANTITY)\n   373→\n   374→      const { error: updateError } = await supabase\n   375→     { status: 500 }\n   384→        )\n   385→      }\n   386→\n   387→      const response = NextResponse.json({ success: true, updated: true })\n   388→      if (needsSessionCookie) {\n   389→ user_id: userId,\n   406→      ...(personalization_id ? { personalization_id } : {}),\n   407→      ...(composition_id ? { composition_id } : {}),\n   408→    }\n   409→\n   410→    const { error: }\n   431→    return response\n   432→  } catch (error) {\n   433→    console.error('Cart API error:', error)\n   434→    return NextResponse.json(\n   435→      { error: 'Internal server error' },\n true, deleted: true })\n   508→      }\n   509→    }\n   510→\n   511→    // Build update payload\n   512→    const updatePayload: Record<string, any> = { updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }\n   513→\n   514→    if (typeof quantity === 'number') {\n   515→      updatePayload.quantity = quantity\n   516→    }\n   517→\n   518→    // Handle variant change\n   519→    if (variant_details && (variant_details.size || variant_details.color)) {\n   520→      // Get the cart item to find its product_id (with ownership check)\n   521→      const cartItemQuery = supabase\n   522→ else if (sessionId) {\n   529→        cartItemQuery.eq('session_id', sessionId)\n   530→      }\n   531→\n   532→      const { data: cartItem } = await cartItemQuery.single()\n   533→\n   534→      if }\n   540→\n   541→      // Resolve new variant_id from variant_details\n   542→      let variantQuery = supabase\n   543→        .from('product_variants')\n   544→        .select('id')\n   545→        .eq('product_id', cartItem.product_id)\n   546→        .eq('is_enabled', true)\n   547→        .eq('is_available', true)\n   548→\n   549→      if (variant_details.size) {\n   550→        variantQuery = variantQuery.eq('size', variant_details.size)\n   551→      }\n   552→      if (variant_details.color) {\n   553→        variantQuery = variantQuery.eq('color', variant_details.color)\n NextResponse.json(\n   560→          { error: 'Variant not available', code: 'VARIANT_UNAVAILABLE' },\n   561→          { status: 400 }\n   562→        )\n   563→      }\n   564→\n   565→      updatePayload.variant_id = matchedVariants[0].id\n   566→    }\n   567→\n   568→    // Must have something to update\n   569→    if (Object.keys(updatePayload).length <= 1) {\n   570→      return NextResponse.json(\n   571→        { error: 'Invalid request', message: 'quantity or variant_details required' },\n   572→        { status: 400 }\n   573→      )\n   574→    }\n   575→\n   576→    // Update cart item (with ownership check)\n   577→    const updateQuery = supabase\n   578→      .from('cart_items')\n   579→      .update(updatePayload)\n   580→      .eq('id', item_id)\n   581→\n   582→    if (userId) {\n   583→      updateQuery.eq('user_id', userId)\n   584→    } else if (sessionId) {\n   585→      updateQuery.eq('session_id', sessionId)\n   586→    }\n   587→\n   588→    const { error } = await updateQuery\n   589→\n   590→    if (error) {\n   591→      console.error('Cart item update error:', error)\n   592→      return NextResponse.json(\n   593→        { error: 'Failed to update cart item', message: error.message },\n   594→        { status: 500 }\n   595→      )\n   596→    }\n   597→\n   598→    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, updated: true })\n   599→ supabase.from('cart_items').delete()\n   626→\n   627→    if (userId) {\n   628→      deleteQuery.eq('user_id', userId)\n   629→    } else if (sessionId) {\n   630→      deleteQuery.eq('session_id', sessionId)\n   631→    } else {\n   632→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'No cart found' }, { status: 404 })\n   633→    }\n   634→\n   635→    const { error } = await deleteQuery\n   636→\n {"parentUuid":"4e731bc9-ee7b-4f54-9932-ebd791d447aa","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a3d5d65c592ec9f5d","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01W9JGUNAsoND6VA4G85RYz8","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Stripe Checkout Session Creation API\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/checkout/create-session\n     5→ * Creates a Stripe Checkout session for payment processing\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';\n     9→import { stripe } from '@/lib/stripe';\n    10→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin';\n    11→import { getProvider, initializeProviders } from '@/lib/pod';\n    12→import { STORE_DEFAULTS, ALLOWED_SHIPPING_COUNTRIES, BASE_URL } from '@/lib/store-config';\n    13→import { LINE_HEIGHT, computeFontSize, getProductionBaseFontSize, CATEGORY_TO_PRODUCT_TYPE } from '@/lib/print-areas';\n    14→import { createCanvas, registerFont } from 'canvas';\n    15→import path from 'path';\n    16→import fs from 'fs';\n    17→\n    18→// Font mapping for personalization\n    19→const FONT_FILES: Record<string, string> = {\n    20→  'Inter': 'Inter-Regular.ttf',\n    21→  'Roboto': 'Roboto-Regular.ttf',\n    22→  'Playfair Display': 'PlayfairDisplay-Regular.ttf',\n    23→  'Montserrat': 'Montserrat-Regular.ttf',\n    24→  'Oswald': 'Oswald-Regular.ttf',\n    25→  'Lato': 'Lato-Regular.ttf',\n    26→  'Pacifico': 'Pacifico-Regular.ttf',\n    27→  'Dancing Script': 'DancingScript-Regular.ttf',\n    28→  'Great Vibes': 'GreatVibes-Regular.ttf',\n    29→  'Caveat': 'Caveat-Regular.ttf',\n    30→  'Permanent Marker': 'PermanentMarker-Regular.ttf',\n    31→  'Bebas Neue': 'BebasNeue-Regular.ttf',\n    32→};\n    33→\n    34→// Register all fonts once at module load\n    35→Object.entries(FONT_FILES).forEach(([fontName, fileName]) => {\n    36→  const fontPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'public', 'fonts', fileName);\n    37→  if (fs.existsSync(fontPath)) {\n    38→    registerFont(fontPath, { family: fontName });\n    39→  }\n    40→});\n    41→\n    42→/**\n    43→ * Generate a high-resolution PNG with personalization text\n    44→ * Returns base64 string (without data:image/png;base64, prefix)\n    45→ */\n    46→function generatePersonalizationPNG(\n    47→  text: string,\n    48→  font: string,\n    49→  fontSize: number,\n    50→  fontColor: string,\n    51→  width: number,\n    52→  height: number\n    53→): string {\n    54→  const canvas = createCanvas(width, height);\n    55→  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    56→\n    57→  // Transparent background\n    58→  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n    59→\n    60→  // Set font properties\n    61→  const selectedFont = FONT_FILES[font] ? font : 'Inter';\n    62→  ctx.font = `${fontSize}px \"${selectedFont}\"`;\n    63→  ctx.fillStyle = fontColor;\n    64→  ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n    65→  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n    66→\n    67→  // Render multi-line text\n    68→  const lineHeight = fontSize * 1.3;\n    69→  const textLines = text.split('\\n');\n    70→  const totalHeight = textLines.length * lineHeight;\n    71→  const startY = (height / 2) - (totalHeight / 2) + (lineHeight / 2);\n    72→\n    73→  textLines.forEach((line, index) => {\n    74→    const y = startY + (index * lineHeight);\n    75→    ctx.fillText(line, width / 2, y);\n    76→  });\n    77→\n    78→  // Convert to base64 (without data URL prefix)\n    79→  const buffer = canvas.toBuffer('image/png');\n    80→  return buffer.toString('base64');\n    81→}\n    82→\n    83→/**\n    84→ * POST /api/checkout/create-session\n    85→ *\n    86→ * Create a Stripe Checkout Session for cart checkout\n    87→ *\n    88→ * Request body:\n    89→ * @param {Array} cartItems - Array of cart items with productId, variantId, quantity, personalization\n    90→ * @param {Object} shippingAddress - Shipping address (name, line1, city, state, postal_code, country)\n    91→ * @param {string} locale - User locale (en/es/de, default: en)\n    92→ * @param {string} currency - Currency code (default: EUR)\n    93→ * @param {string} customerEmail - Customer email address\n    94→ * @param {string} gift_message - Optional gift message\n    95→ *\n    96→ * @returns {Object} JSON response with Stripe Checkout Session URL\n    97→ */\n    98→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    99→  try {\n   100→    initializeProviders();\n   101→    const provider = getProvider();\n   102→\n   103→    const body = await req.json();\n   104→    const { cartItems, shippingAddress, locale = 'en', currency = STORE_DEFAULTS.stripeCurrency, customerEmail, gift_message, couponCode } = body;\n   105→\n   106→    // Validate required fields\n   107→    if (!cartItems || !Array.isArray(cartItems) || cartItems.length === 0) {\n   108→      return NextResponse.json(\n   109→        { error: 'Cart items are required' },\n   110→        { status: 400 }\n   111→      );\n   112→    }\n   113→\n   114→    // Validate all items have variant_id (required for Printify fulfillment)\n   115→    const itemsWithoutVariant = cartItems.filter(\n   116→      (item: any) => !item.variant_id\n   117→    );\n   118→    if (itemsWithoutVariant.length > 0) {\n   119→      return NextResponse.json(\n   120→        {\n   121→          error: 'Incomplete cart items',\n   122→          code: 'MISSING_VARIANTS',\n   123→          message: 'All items require a variant selection (size/color).',\n   124→          items: itemsWithoutVariant.map((i: any) => i.product_id),\n   125→        },\n   126→        { status: 400 }\n   127→      );\n   128→    }\n   129→\n   130→    // Validate all products exist and are active\n   131→    const productIds = [...new Set(cartItems.map((item: any) => item.product_id).filter(Boolean))];\n   132→\n   133→    // Verify all products are active\n   134→    if (productIds.length > 0) {\n   135→      const { data: activeProducts } = await supabaseAdmin\n   136→        .from('products')\n   137→        .select('id')\n   138→        .in('id', productIds)\n   139→        .eq('status', 'active')\n   140→        .is('deleted_at', null);\n   141→\n   142→      const activeProductIds = new Set((activeProducts || []).map((p: any) => p.id));\n   143→      const inactiveItems = productIds.filter(id => !activeProductIds.has(id));\n   144→      if (inactiveItems.length > 0) {\n   145→        return NextResponse.json(\n   146→          {\n   147→            error: 'Some products are no longer available',\n   148→            code: 'PRODUCTS_UNAVAILABLE',\n   149→            items: inactiveItems,\n   150→          },\n   151→          { status: 409 }\n   152→        );\n   153→      }\n   154→    }\n   155→\n   156→    // Stock validation: check variant availability before creating payment session\n   157→    if (productIds.length > 0) {\n   158→      const { data: variants } = await supabaseAdmin\n   159→        .from('product_variants')\n   160→        .select('product_id, color, size, is_available, price_cents')\n   161→        .in('product_id', productIds)\n   162→        .eq('is_enabled', true);\n   163→\n   164→      const unavailableItems: Array<{ productId: string; color?: string; size?: string; name?: string }> = [];\n   165→\n   166→      for (const item of cartItems) {\n   167→        if (!item.product_id) continue;\n   168→        const itemColor = item.variant_details?.color || item.color;\n   169→        const itemSize = item.variant_details?.size || item.size;\n   170→\n   171→        // Find matching variant\n   172→        const matchingVariant = variants?.find(v =>\n   173→          v.product_id === item.product_id &&\n   174→          (!itemColor || v.color === itemColor) &&\n   175→          (!itemSize || v.size === itemSize)\n   176→        );\n   177→\n   178→        // If no variant found or variant is unavailable\n   179→        if (!matchingVariant || !matchingVariant.is_available) {\n   180→          unavailableItems.push({\n   181→            productId: item.product_id,\n   182→            color: itemColor,\n   183→            size: itemSize,\n   184→            name: item.product_name || item.name,\n   185→          });\n   186→        }\n   187→      }\n   188→\n   189→      if (unavailableItems.length > 0) {\n   190→        return NextResponse.json(\n   191→          {\n   192→            error: 'ITEMS_UNAVAILABLE',\n   193→            unavailableItems,\n   194→          },\n   195→          { status: 409 }\n   196→        );\n   197→      }\n   198→    }\n   199→\n   200→    // --- Coupon validation ---\n   201→    let validatedCoupon: { code: string; discount_type: string; discount_value: number } | null = null;\n   202→    if (couponCode && typeof couponCode === 'string') {\n   203→      const { data: coupon } = await supabaseAdmin\n   204→        .from('coupons')\n   205→        .select('code, discount_type, discount_value, active, min_purchase_amount, usage_limit, times_used')\n   206→        .eq('code', couponCode.trim().toUpperCase())\n   207→        .eq('active', true)\n   208→        .single();\n   209→\n   210→      if (coupon) {\n   211→        const cartTotalForValidation = cartItems.reduce(\n   212→          (sum: number, item: any) => sum + ((item.product_price || 0) * (item.quantity || 1)),\n   213→          0\n   214→        );\n   215→        const meetsMinimum = !coupon.min_purchase_amount || cartTotalForValidation >= coupon.min_purchase_amount;\n   216→        const hasUsesLeft = !coupon.usage_limit || (coupon.times_used || 0) < coupon.usage_limit;\n   217→        if (meetsMinimum && hasUsesLeft) {\n   218→          validatedCoupon = {\n   219→            code: coupon.code,\n   220→            discount_type: coupon.discount_type,\n   221→            discount_value: coupon.discount_value,\n   222→          };\n   223→        }\n   224→      }\n   225→    }\n   226→\n   227→    // --- Personalization: create Printify temp products for personalized items ---\n   228→    const yMap: Record<string, number> = { top: 0.15, center: 0.5, bottom: 0.85 }\n   229→    // Compute production font sizes proportionally using print-areas constants\n   230→    const productionBase = getProductionBaseFontSize('tshirt') // default base\n   231→    const sizeMap: Record<string, number> = {\n   232→      small: computeFontSize('small', productionBase),\n   233→      medium: computeFontSize('medium', productionBase),\n   234→      large: computeFontSize('large', productionBase),\n   235→    }\n   236→\n   237→    for (const item of cartItems) {\n   238→      const personalizationId = item.personalization_id\n   239→      if (!personalizationId) continue\n   240→\n   241→      try {\n   242→        // Load personalization from DB\n   243→        const { data: pz } = await supabaseAdmin\n   244→          .from('personalizations')\n   245→          .select('*')\n   246→          .eq('id', personalizationId)\n   247→          .single()\n   248→\n   249→        if (!pz || !pz.text_content) continue\n   250→        // Skip if already has a temp product (check both old and new columns)\n   251→        if (pz.printify_temp_product_id || pz.provider_temp_product_id) continue\n   252→\n   253→        // Load product from DB (dual-read: provider-agnostic + legacy columns)\n   254→        const { data: dbProduct } = await supabaseAdmin\n   255→          .from('products')\n   256→          .select('id, printify_id, provider_product_id, blueprint_id, product_template_id, print_provider_id, provider_facility_id, pod_provider, title')\n   257→          .eq('id', pz.product_id)\n   258→          .is('deleted_at', null)\n   259→          .single()\n   260→\n   261→        const productExternalId = dbProduct?.provider_product_id || dbProduct?.printify_id\n   262→        const templateId = dbProduct?.product_template_id || dbProduct?.blueprint_id\n   263→        const facilityId = dbProduct?.provider_facility_id || dbProduct?.print_provider_id\n   264→        if (!productExternalId || !templateId || !facilityId) continue\n   265→\n   266→        // Get original product from provider\n   267→        const printifyProduct = await provider.getProduct(productExternalId)\n   268→        const rawProduct = (printifyProduct._raw || printifyProduct) as any\n   269→        const originalPrintAreas = rawProduct.print_areas || []\n   270→\n   271→        // Get placeholder dimensions from the first front placeholder\n   272→        let placeholderWidth = 3000 // Default high-res width\n   273→        let placeholderHeight = 3000 // Default high-res height\n   274→        const frontPlaceholder = originalPrintAreas[0]?.placeholders?.find((p: any) => p.position === 'front')\n   275→        if (frontPlaceholder?.width && frontPlaceholder?.height) {\n   276→          placeholderWidth = frontPlaceholder.width\n   277→          placeholderHeight = frontPlaceholder.height\n   278→        }\n   279→\n   280→        // Generate high-resolution PNG with personalization text\n   281→        const fontSize = sizeMap[pz.font_size || 'medium'] ?? 96\n   282→        const base64PNG = generatePersonalizationPNG(\n   283→          pz.text_content,\n   284→          pz.font_family || 'Inter',\n   285→          fontSize,\n   286→          pz.font_color || '#000000',\n   287→          placeholderWidth,\n   288→          placeholderHeight\n   289→        )\n   290→\n   291→        // Upload PNG to Printify\n   292→        const uploadResult = await provider.uploadDesign({\n   293→          base64: base64PNG,\n   294→          fileName: `personalization-${personalizationId}.png`,\n   295→        })\n   296→\n   297→        // Build modified print_areas with uploaded image\n   298→        const modifiedPrintAreas = originalPrintAreas.map((area: any) => {\n   299→          const modifiedPlaceholders = area.placeholders.map((placeholder: any) => {\n   300→            if (placeholder.position === 'front') {\n   301→              const images = [...(placeholder.images || [])]\n     1→/**\n     2→ * Product Personalization API\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/designs/personalize\n     5→ * Saves a text personalization to the database (draft status).\n     6→ * The actual Printify temp product is created at checkout time.\n     7→ *\n     8→ * AUTH: Anonymous users CAN personalize (user_id = null).\n     9→ * Authenticated users get their user_id linked automatically.\n    10→ * Only AI image generation (/api/designs/generate) requires auth.\n    11→ */\n    12→\n    13→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n    14→import { createServerClient, createAdminClient } from '@/lib/supabase-server'\n    15→import { containsProfanity, getProfanityErrorMessage } from '@/lib/profanity-filter'\n    16→import { personalizeLimiter } from '@/lib/rate-limit'\n    17→\n    18→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    19→  try {\n    20→    const body = await req.json()\n    21→    const {\n    22→      productId,\n    23→      variantId,\n    24→      text,\n    25→      font = 'Inter',\n    26→      fontColor = '#000000',\n    27→      fontSize = 'medium',\n    28→      position = 'bottom',\n    29→      textAlign = 'center',\n    30→      surchargeAmount,\n    31→    } = body\n    32→\n    33→    // Rate limit check\n    34→    const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown'\n    35→    const identifier = body.userId || ip\n    36→    const { success } = personalizeLimiter.check(`personalize:${identifier}`)\n    37→    if (!success) {\n    38→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Too many requests' }, { status: 429 })\n    39→    }\n    40→\n    41→    if (!productId) {\n    42→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'productId is required' }, { status: 400 })\n    43→    }\n    44→\n    45→    if (!text || typeof text !== 'string' || text.trim().length === 0) {\n    46→      return NextResponse.json(\n    47→        { error: 'Text is required for personalization' },\n    48→        { status: 400 }\n    49→      )\n    50→    }\n    51→\n    52→    // Validate line count and character limits\n    53→    const lines = text.split('\\n');\n    54→    if (lines.length > 3) {\n    55→      return NextResponse.json(\n    56→        { error: 'Maximum 3 lines allowed' },\n    57→        { status: 400 }\n    58→      );\n    59→    }\n    60→\n    61→    // Validate each line is max 50 characters\n    62→    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {\n    63→      if (lines[i].length > 50) {\n    64→        return NextResponse.json(\n    65→          { error: `Line ${i + 1} exceeds 50 characters (${lines[i].length} chars)` },\n    66→          { status: 400 }\n    67→        );\n    68→      }\n    69→    }\n    70→\n    71→    // Check for profanity (server-side validation)\n    72→    if (containsProfanity(text)) {\n    73→      return NextResponse.json(\n    74→        { error: getProfanityErrorMessage() },\n    75→        { status: 400 }\n    76→      )\n    77→    }\n    78→\n    79→    // Validate fontSize and position\n    80→    const validSizes = ['small', 'medium', 'large']\n    81→    const validPositions = ['top', 'center', 'bottom']\n    82→    const validAligns = ['left', 'center', 'right']\n    83→    if (!validSizes.includes(fontSize)) {\n    84→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid fontSize' }, { status: 400 })\n    85→    }\n    86→    if (!validPositions.includes(position)) {\n    87→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid position' }, { status: 400 })\n    88→    }\n    89→    if (!validAligns.includes(textAlign)) {\n    90→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid textAlign' }, { status: 400 })\n    91→    }\n    92→\n    93→    // Try to get authenticated user (optional — anonymous users can personalize)\n    94→    const { user } = await createServerClient(req)\n    95→\n    96→    // Use admin client to insert (bypasses RLS so anonymous users can save)\n    97→    const adminClient = createAdminClient()\n    98→\n    99→    // Verify product exists\n   100→    const { data: product, error: productError } = await adminClient\n   101→      .from('products')\n   102→      .select('id')\n   103→      .eq('id', productId)\n   104→      .single()\n   105→\n   106→    if (productError || !product) {\n   107→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Product not found' }, { status: 404 })\n   108→    }\n   109→\n   110→    // Save personalization to DB as draft\n   111→    // user_id is null for anonymous users, linked at checkout when they authenticate\n   112→    const { data: personalization, error: saveError } = await adminClient\n   113→      .from('personalizations')\n   114→      .insert({\n   115→        user_id: user?.id || null,\n   116→        product_id: productId,\n   117→        variant_id: variantId || null,\n   118→        text_content: text.trim(),\n   119→        font_family: font,\n   120→        font_color: fontColor,\n   121→        font_size: fontSize,\n   122→        position: position,\n   123→        text_align: textAlign,\n   124→        surcharge_amount: surchargeAmount ?? null,\n   125→        printify_temp_product_id: null,\n   126→        provider_temp_product_id: null,\n   127→        status: 'draft',\n   128→      })\n   129→      .select('id')\n   130→      .single()\n   131→\n   132→    if (saveError) {\n   133→      console.error('Failed to save personalization:', saveError)\n   134→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to save personalization' }, { status: 500 })\n   135→    }\n   136→\n   137→    return NextResponse.json({\n   138→      success: true,\n   139→      personalizationId: personalization.id,\n   140→    })\n   141→  } catch (error) {\n   142→    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'\n   143→    console.error('Personalization error:', message)\n   144→    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 500 })\n   145→  }\n   146→}\n   147→"}]},"uuid":"52f75c31-8865-4ec3-af2c-aacbc279990a","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:28.900Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"72329e19-dcf5-433c-a992-5425743a92af"}

Supports preview (1024x1024) and\n     6→ * production-resolution output (per PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS).\n     7→ */\n     8→\n     9→import sharp from 'sharp'\n    10→import { createCanvas, registerFont } from 'canvas'\n    11→import path from 'path'\n    12→import fs from 'fs'\n    13→import { PRINT_AREAS, PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS, PrintArea } from '@/lib/print-areas'\n    14→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n    15→\n    16→// ── Font registration ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    17→\n    18→const FONT_FILES: Record<string, string> = {\n    19→  'Inter': 'Inter-Regular.ttf',\n    20→  'Roboto': 'Roboto-Regular.ttf',\n    21→  'Playfair Display': 'PlayfairDisplay-Regular.ttf',\n    22→  'Montserrat': 'Montserrat-Regular.ttf',\n    23→  'Oswald': 'Oswald-Regular.ttf',\n    24→  'Lato': 'Lato-Regular.ttf',\n    25→  'Pacifico': 'Pacifico-Regular.ttf',\n    26→  'Dancing Script': 'DancingScript-Regular.ttf',\n    27→  'Great Vibes': 'GreatVibes-Regular.ttf',\n    28→  'Caveat': 'Caveat-Regular.ttf',\n    29→  'Permanent Marker': 'PermanentMarker-Regular.ttf',\n    30→  'Bebas Neue': 'BebasNeue-Regular.ttf',\n    31→}\n    32→\n    33→let fontsRegistered = false\n    34→\n    35→function ensureFontsRegistered(): void {\n    36→  if (fontsRegistered) return\n    37→  Object.entries(FONT_FILES).forEach(([fontName, fileName]) => {\n    38→    const fontPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'public', 'fonts', fileName)\n    39→    if (fs.existsSync(fontPath)) {\n    40→      try {\n    41→        registerFont(fontPath, { family: fontName })\n    42→      } catch {\n    43→        // Font may already be registered\n    44→      }\n    45→    }\n    46→  })\n    47→  fontsRegistered = true\n    48→}\n    49→\n    50→// ── Layer types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    51→\n    52→export interface CompositionLayer {\n    53→  type: 'text' | 'image' | 'ai'\n    54→  url?: string\n    55→  text?: string\n    56→  font?: string\n    57→  color?: string\n    58→  fontSize?: string // 'small' | 'medium' | 'large' or pixel value\n    59→  position?: string // 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom'\n    60→  textAlign?: string // 'left' | 'center' | 'right'\n    61→}\n    62→\n    63→// ── Position mapping ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    64→\n    65→const POSITION_Y_RATIO: Record<string, number> = {\n    66→  top: 0.15,\n    67→  center: 0.5,\n    68→  bottom: 0.85,\n    69→}\n    70→\n    71→// ── Font size parsing ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    72→\n    73→function parseFontSize(fontSize: string | undefined, baseHeight: number): number {\n    74→  if (!fontSize) return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.08) // default: 8% of height\n    75→  if (fontSize === 'small') return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.05)\n    76→  if (fontSize === 'medium') return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.08)\n    77→  if (fontSize === 'large') return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.12)\n    78→  // Try parsing as numeric pixel value\n    79→  const parsed = parseInt(fontSize, 10)\n    80→  if (!isNaN(parsed) && parsed > 0) return parsed\n    81→  return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.08)\n    82→}\n    83→\n    84→// ── Text rendering via canvas ────────────────────────────────────────\n    85→\n    86→function renderTextToBuffer(\n    87→  text: string,\n    88→  font: string,\n    89→  fontSize: number,\n    90→  color: string,\n    91→  width: number,\n    92→  height: number,\n    93→  textAlign: CanvasTextAlign = 'center',\n    94→  positionY: number = 0.5\n    95→): Buffer {\n    96→  ensureFontsRegistered()\n    97→\n    98→  const canvas = createCanvas(width, height)\n    99→  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')\n   100→\n   101→  // Transparent background\n   102→  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height)\n   103→\n   104→  // Resolve font family\n   105→  const selectedFont = FONT_FILES[font] ? font : 'Inter'\n   106→  ctx.font = `${fontSize}px \"${selectedFont}\"`\n   107→  ctx.fillStyle = color || '#000000'\n   108→  ctx.textAlign = textAlign\n   109→  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'\n   110→\n   111→  // Calculate x position based on alignment\n   112→  let x: number\n   113→  switch (textAlign) {\n   114→    case 'left':\n   115→      x = Math.round(width * 0.05) // 5% margin\n   116→      break\n   117→    case 'right':\n   118→      x = Math.round(width * 0.95)\n   119→      break\n   120→    default:\n   121→      x = Math.round(width / 2)\n   122→  }\n   123→\n   124→  // Multi-line text rendering\n   125→  const lineHeight = fontSize * 1.3\n   126→  const textLines = text.split('\\n')\n   127→  const totalTextHeight = textLines.length * lineHeight\n   128→  const startY = Math.round(height * positionY - totalTextHeight / 2 + lineHeight / 2)\n   129→\n   130→  textLines.forEach((line, index) => {\n   131→    const y = startY + index * lineHeight\n   132→    ctx.fillText(line, x, y)\n   133→  })\n   134→\n   135→  return canvas.toBuffer('image/png')\n   136→}\n   137→\n   138→// ── Preview rendering ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   139→\n   140→/**\n   141→ * Render a multi-layer design composition as a 1024x1024 preview PNG.\n   142→ *\n   143→ * @param layers - Array of composition layers (text, image, ai)\n   144→ * @param productType - Product type key (tshirt, hoodie, mug, etc.)\n   145→ * @returns PNG buffer of the composed preview\n   146→ */\n   147→export async function renderCompositionPreview(\n   148→  layers: CompositionLayer[],\n   149→  productType: string\n   150→): Promise<Buffer> {\n   151→  const CANVAS_SIZE = 1024\n   152→  const area: PrintArea = PRINT_AREAS[productType] || PRINT_AREAS['tshirt']\n   153→\n   154→  // Start with a transparent 1024x1024 canvas\n   155→  let composite = sharp({\n   156→    create: {\n   157→      width: CANVAS_SIZE,\n   158→      height: CANVAS_SIZE,\n   159→      channels: 4,\n   160→      background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 },\n   161→    },\n   162→  }).png()\n   163→\n   164→  const compositeInputs: sharp.OverlayOptions[] = []\n   165→\n   166→  for (const layer of layers) {\n   167→    try {\n   168→      if (layer.type === 'image' || layer.type === 'ai') {\n   169→        if (!layer.url) continue\n   170→\n   171→        // Fetch the image\n   172→        const response = await fetch(layer.url)\n   173→        if (!response.ok) {\n   174→          console.error(`Failed to fetch layer image: ${response.status} ${layer.url}`)\n   175→          continue\n   176→        }\n   177→        const imageBuffer = Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer())\n   178→\n   179→        // Resize to fit print area\n   180→        const resized = await sharp(imageBuffer)\n   181→          .resize(area.w, area.h, {\n   182→            fit: 'contain',\n   183→            background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 },\n   184→          })\n   185→          .png()\n   186→          .toBuffer()\n   187→\n   188→        compositeInputs.push({\n   189→          input: resized,\n   190→          left: area.x,\n   191→          top: area.y,\n   192→          blend: 'over',\n   193→        })\n   194→      } else if (layer.type === 'text') {\n   195→        if (!layer.text) continue\n   196→\n   197→        const fontSize = parseFontSize(layer.fontSize, area.h)\n   198→        const positionY = POSITION_Y_RATIO[layer.position || 'center'] ?? 0.5\n   199→        const textAlign = (layer.textAlign as CanvasTextAlign) || 'center'\n   200→"}]},"uuid":"e8e13ab7-fbe8-4f9f-adc1-0569c7fdf547","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:34.273Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0d96ca03-eed6-4567-8cb7-13aadb032973"}

exportForProduction(compositionId: string): Promise<string> {\n   244→  // Load composition from DB\n   245→  const { data: composition, error } = await supabaseAdmin\n   246→ Error(`Composition not found: ${compositionId}`)\n   253→  }\n   254→\n   255→  // If already exported, return existing URL\n   256→  if (composition.production_url) {\n   257→    return composition.production_url\n   258→  }\n   259→\n   260→  const productType = composition.product_type || 'tshirt'\n   261→  // Schema v2: Fabric.js JSON — client exports production PNG directly\n   262→  if (composition.schema_version === 2) {\n   263→    // For Fabric.js compositions, the client exports the final PNG.\n   264→    // Production export is handled client-side via canvas.toDataURL() with multiplier.\n   265→    // If a production_url is already set (from client export), return it.\n   266→    if (composition.production_url) {\n   267→      return composition.production_url\n   268→    }\n   269→    // If no production_url yet, use the preview as fallback\n   270→    if (composition.preview_url) {\n   271→      return composition.preview_url\n   272→    }\n   273→    throw new Error(`Fabric.js composition ${compositionId} has no production or preview URL`)\n   274→  }\n   275→\n   276→  const layers: CompositionLayer[] = composition.layers || []\n   277→  const prodDims = PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS[productType] || PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS['tshirt']\n   278→  const previewArea = PRINT_AREAS[productType] || PRINT_AREAS['tshirt']\n   279→\n   280→  // Calculate scale factor from preview (1024) to production dimensions\n   281→  const scaleX = prodDims.w / previewArea.w\n   282→  const scaleY = prodDims.h / previewArea.h\n   283→\n   284→  // Production canvas is just the print area at production resolution\n   285→  const prodWidth = prodDims.w\n   286→  const prodHeight = prodDims.h\n   287→\n   288→  let composite = sharp({\n   289→    create: {\n   290→      width: prodWidth,\n   291→      height: prodHeight,\n   292→      channels: 4,\n   293→      background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 },\n   294→    },\n   295→  }).png()\n   296→\n   297→  const compositeInputs: sharp.OverlayOptions[] = []\n   298→\n   299→  for (const layer of layers) {\n   300→    try {\n   301→      if (layer.type === 'image' || layer.type === 'ai') {\n   302→        if (!layer.url) continue\n   303→\n   304→        const response = await fetch(layer.url)\n   305→        if (!response.ok) continue\n   306→        const imageBuffer = Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer())\n   307→\n   308→        // Resize to fill production dimensions\n   309→        const resized = await sharp(imageBuffer)\n   310→          .resize(prodWidth, prodHeight, {\n   311→            fit: 'contain',\n   312→            background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 },\n   313→          })\n   314→          .png()\n   315→          .toBuffer()\n   316→\n   317→        compositeInputs.push({\n   318→          input: resized,\n   319→          left: 0,\n   320→          top: 0,\n   321→          blend: 'over',\n   322→        })\n   323→      } else if (layer.type === 'text') {\n   324→        if (!layer.text) continue\n   325→\n   326→        // Scale font size to production resolution\n   327→        const previewFontSize = parseFontSize(layer.fontSize, previewArea.h)\n   328→        const prodFontSize = Math.round(previewFontSize * scaleY)\n   329→        const positionY = POSITION_Y_RATIO[layer.position || 'center'] ?? 0.5\n   330→        const textAlign = (layer.textAlign as CanvasTextAlign) || 'center'\n   331→\n   332→        const textBuffer = renderTextToBuffer(\n   333→          layer.text,\n   334→          layer.font || 'Inter',\n   335→          prodFontSize,\n   336→          layer.color || '#000000',\n   337→          prodWidth,\n   338→          prodHeight,\n   339→          textAlign,\n   340→          positionY\n   341→        )\n   342→\n   343→        compositeInputs.push({\n   344→          input: textBuffer,\n   345→          left: 0,\n   346→          top: 0,\n   347→          blend: 'over',\n   348→        })\n   349→      }\n   350→    } catch (layerError) {\n   351→      console.error('Error processing production layer:', layerError)\n   352→    }\n   353→  }\n   354→\n   355→  if (compositeInputs.length > 0) {\n   356→    composite = composite.composite(compositeInputs)\n   357→  }\n   358→\n   359→  const productionBuffer = await composite.png().toBuffer()\n   360→\n   361→  // Upload to Supabase Storage bucket 'designs'\n   362→  const filename = `compositions/${compositionId}/production.png`\n   363→  const { error: uploadError } = await supabaseAdmin.storage\n   364→    .from('designs')\n   365→    .upload(filename, productionBuffer, {\n   366→      contentType: 'image/png',\n   367→      cacheControl: '31536000',\n   368→      upsert: true,\n   369→    })\n   370→\n   371→  if (uploadError) {\n   372→    console.error('Production upload error:', uploadError)\n   373→    // Fallback: return as base64 data URL\n   374→    const base64 = productionBuffer.toString('base64')\n   375→    const dataUrl = `data:image/png;base64,${base64}`\n   376→    return dataUrl\n   377→  }\n   378→\n   379→  const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabaseAdmin.storage\n   380→    .from('designs')\n   381→    .getPublicUrl(filename)\n   382→\n   383→  // Update composition record with production URL\n   384→  await supabaseAdmin\n   385→ {"parentUuid":"9724c43c-63c1-44a5-b815-4e5d77439841","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a3d5d65c592ec9f5d","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BPZrdp1uAwCRDCXyS3CZ83","type":"tool_result","content":"    variantColor,\n  } = useDesignEditor()\n\n  const { save, load } = useDesignPersistence()\n\n  const { saveState, undo, redo } = useCanvasHistory(\n    fabricCanvasRef,\n    (canUndo, canRedo) => setHistoryState(canUndo, canRedo)\n  )\n\n  // Determine available colors: from real variants if available, else from templates\n  const availableColors = (variants?.colors && variants.colors.length > 0)\n    ? variants.colors\n    : (TEMPLATE_COLORS[product.productType] || ['white'])\n\n  // Initialize product in store\n  useEffect(() => {\n    initProduct({\n      id: product.id,\n      title: product.title,\n      category: product.category,\n--\n  // Garment color change\n  const handleColorChange = useCallback((color: string) => {\n    // Serialize current panel before color change (canvas will re-init)\n    if (canvasRef.current) {\n      const currentJson = canvasRef.current.exportJSON()\n      setPanelState(activePanel, {\n        fabricJson: currentJson,\n        isDirty: isDirty,\n      })\n    }\n    setVariantColor(color)\n  }, [activePanel, isDirty, setPanelState, setVariantColor])\n\n  // History save handler\n  const handleHistorySave = useCallback(() => {\n    saveState()\n  }, [saveState])","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"e34877dd-98f4-4920-9b8f-206b12167b51","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:53.639Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9724c43c-63c1-44a5-b815-4e5d77439841"}
