Now I have a comprehensive view. Let me compile my findings:\n\n## DESIGN PERSISTENCE & CART INTEGRATION AUDIT\n\nBased on my thorough analysis of the codebase, here is a complete audit report with schema diagram, data flow, and identified bugs/gaps:\n\n---\n\n## SCHEMA DIAGRAM\n\n```\nDESIGN_COMPOSITIONS TABLE\n├── id (UUID, PK)\n├── user_id (UUID, FK → auth.users)\n├── session_id (UUID, FK → design_sessions, optional)\n├── product_id (UUID, FK → products, optional)\n├── product_type (TEXT: 'tshirt', 'hoodie', etc.)\n├── schema_version (INTEGER: 1, 2, or 3)\n├── layers (JSONB)\n│   ├── [schema_v1] CompositionLayer[] (from /api/designs/compose)\n│   │   └── { type, url, text, font, color, fontSize, position, textAlign }\n│   ├── [schema_v2] Fabric.js JSON (from DesignStudioPage via compose-v2)\n│   └── [schema_v3] { panels: Record<panel, { fabricJson, previewDataUrl? }>, schema_version: 3 }\n├── preview_url (TEXT: Supabase Storage URL)\n├── production_url (TEXT: Supabase Storage URL, set by exportForProduction)\n├── surcharge_amount (DECIMAL)\n├── status (TEXT: 'draft', 'applied', 'ordered')\n├── created_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)\n└── updated_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)\n\nCART_ITEMS TABLE\n├── id (UUID, PK)\n├── user_id (UUID, FK, optional)\n├── session_id (UUID, optional)\n├── product_id (UUID, FK)\n├── quantity (INTEGER)\n├── variant_id (UUID, FK → product_variants)\n├── personalization_id (UUID, FK → personalizations, optional)\n├── composition_id (UUID, FK → design_compositions, optional) [20260228200900]\n├── created_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)\n└── updated_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)\n\nKEY INSIGHT: composition_id links a cart item to a specific design composition.\nThe variantColor (selected garment color) is NOT persisted in design_compositions!\n```\n\n---\n\n## DATA FLOW - DESIGN SAVE TO CHECKOUT\n\n### 1. DESIGN SAVE FLOW (DesignStudioPage:180-223)\n\n```\nUser saves design in editor\n    ↓\nhandleSave() called\n    ├─ Serialize current active panel: canvasRef.exportJSON()\n    ├─ Merge with all panelStates from Zustand\n    ├─ Build payload:\n    │   {\n    │     fabricJson: { \n    │       panels: Record<panel, { fabricJson, previewDataUrl? }>,\n    │       schema_version: 3\n    │     },\n    │     previewDataUrl: PNG base64,\n    │     productType: 'tshirt',\n    │     productId: UUID,\n    │     compositionId: UUID (if updating) || undefined\n    │   }\n    │\n    ├─ POST /api/designs/compose-v2\n    │   ├─ Auth: createServerClient(request)\n    │   ├─ Generate composition ID: crypto.randomUUID()\n    │   ├─ Upload preview PNG to Supabase Storage: /designs/compositions/{id}/preview.png\n    │   ├─ Upsert design_compositions:\n    │   │   {\n    │   │     id, user_id, schema_version: 2, \n    │   │     layers: fabricJson, \n    │   │     product_type, product_id,\n    │   │     preview_url, status: 'draft',\n    │   │     updated_at: now()\n    │   │   }\n    │   │\n    │   └─ Return: { composition_id, preview_url }\n    │\n    ├─ setCompositionId(result.composition_id)\n    ├─ setDirty(false)\n    └─ toast.success('Saved')\n\nCRITICAL BUG #1: variantColor is NOT saved!\nThe selected garment color (e.g., \"red\", \"white\") exists only in Zustand state:\n  useDesignEditor.variantColor\nBut when the composition is saved to design_compositions.layers, \n  NO COLOR INFORMATION is persisted.\nIf user closes editor and reopens: the color reverts to default (first available color).\n\nCRITICAL BUG #2: Multi-panel state is only partially implemented\n  ├─ handleSave() serializes panelStates correctly\n  ├─ BUT compose-v2 API sets schema_version: 2 (legacy!)\n  └─ Should be schema_version: 3 for multi-panel\n  \n  Line 63 in /api/designs/compose-v2:\n    schema_version: 2,\n  \n  Should be:\n    schema_version: 3,  (when panels.length > 1)\n    OR dynamically detect from fabricJson\n```\n\n### 2. APPLY TO CART FLOW (DesignStudioPage:226-237)\n\n```\nUser clicks \"Apply to Cart\"\n    ↓\nhandleApplyToCart()\n    ├─ If isDirty || !compositionId:\n    │   └─ Call handleSave() first ← waits for async save\n    │\n    ├─ Get latest compositionId from Zustand\n    └─ Navigate: /shop/{productId}?compositionId={compositionId}\n       ↓\n       ProductDetailClient renders (line 98)\n       ├─ compositionIdParam = searchParams.get('compositionId')\n       │\n       └─ handleAddToCart() [line 267-327]\n           ├─ Save personalization if exists\n           ├─ Call addToCart(\n           │     product.id,\n           │     quantity,\n           │     variant_details: { size, color },  ← User's CURRENT SELECTION\n           │     ...,\n           │     compositionId  ← From URL param\n           │   )\n           │\n           └─ POST /api/cart\n               ├─ Lookup/create variant_id from size + color\n               ├─ Insert cart_item:\n               │   {\n               │     product_id, quantity, variant_id,\n               │     composition_id,  ← Stored here\n               │     personalization_id,\n               │     ...\n               │   }\n               │\n               └─ Return: { success: true, added: true }\n\nBUG #3: Mismatch between design color and order color\n  ├─ Design saved with NO color info\n  ├─ User adds to cart with selectedColor from ProductDetailClient\n  ├─ These are INDEPENDENT!\n  │   ├─ Design showed red garment + custom artwork\n  │   ├─ User then changes color to blue in product detail page\n  │   ├─ Cart item is added with BLUE variant\n  │   ├─ But composition was designed on RED!\n  │   └─ Result: WRONG MOCKUP AT CHECKOUT\n```\n\n### 3. CHECKOUT FLOW (create-session/route.ts:369-481)\n\n```\nUser proceeds to checkout\n    ↓\nPOST /api/checkout/create-session\n    │\n    ├─ For each cart item:\n    │   └─ If item.composition_id:\n    │       ├─ exportForProduction(compositionId)\n    │       │   ├─ Load composition from DB\n    │       │   ├─ If schema_version === 2 (Fabric.js):\n    │       │   │   ├─ Check if production_url already set\n    │       │   │   ├─ If yes: return it (CACHED)\n    │       │   │   └─ If no: throw error (client should export)\n    │       │   │\n    │       │   └─ If schema_version === 1 (old CompositionLayer[]):\n    │       │       ├─ Render layers to production resolution\n    │       │       ├─ Upload to Supabase Storage: /designs/compositions/{id}/production.png\n    │       │       └─ Update composition.production_url\n    │       │\n    │       ├─ Fetch production image (URL or base64)\n    │       ├─ Upload to Printify via provider.uploadDesign()\n    │       ├─ Load original product from Printify\n    │       ├─ Build modified print_areas with composition image\n    │       ├─ Create temp product in Printify\n    │       ├─ Update composition status: 'draft' → 'ordered'\n    │       └─ Attach temp product ID to cart item for Stripe metadata\n    │\n    ├─ Build Stripe line items\n    │   └─ metadata.cart_items = JSON.stringify([\n    │       { product_id, variant_id, quantity, \n    │         composition_id, personalization_id, \n    │         printify_temp_id }\n    │     ])\n    │\n    └─ Create Stripe session → return { sessionId, url }\n\nBUG #4: Production export has version mismatch\n  ├─ If schema_version === 2 (Fabric.js):\n  │   ├─ exportForProduction() expects client-exported production_url\n  │   ├─ But client only exports preview_url\n  │   └─ Result: If production_url is NULL, throws error\n  │       \"Fabric.js composition {id} has no production or preview URL\"\n  │\n  └─ Fallback: Uses preview_url (1024x1024) instead of production res!\n     Prints at LOW resolution with blurry text/images.\n```\n\n---\n\n## CRITICAL BUGS & GAPS\n\n### BUG #1: variantColor NOT PERSISTED ⚠️ SEVERITY: CRITICAL\n\n**Files:**\n- `frontend/src/components/design-studio/DesignStudioPage.tsx:156-167`\n- `frontend/src/hooks/useDesignEditor.ts:69` (variantColor exists only in Zustand)\n- `frontend/src/app/api/designs/compose-v2/route.ts:14` (payload has no color field)\n\n**Problem:**\n- User designs on red garment with custom artwork\n- Saves composition → `design_compositions.layers` contains ONLY Fabric.js JSON\n- variantColor (\"red\") stored only in Zustand state, never sent to API\n- User closes editor\n- Later: User opens saved composition → reverts to first available color (default)\n- Design intent lost\n\n**Evidence:**\n```typescript\n// compose-v2 request (line 14 in DesignStudioPage)\nconst body = JSON.stringify({\n  fabricJson, previewDataUrl, productType, productId, compositionId\n})\n// NO variantColor field!\n\n// Zustand store (useDesignEditor.ts:69)\nvariantColor: string  // ← only in memory, never persisted\n```\n\n**Impact:** Orders print on wrong color garment\n\n**Fix Required:**\n```typescript\n// Add to compose-v2 payload:\n{\n  fabricJson: { panels, schema_version: 3 },\n  previewDataUrl,\n  productType,\n  productId,\n  variantColor,  // ← ADD THIS\n  compositionId\n}\n\n// Update design_compositions schema:\nALTER TABLE design_compositions ADD COLUMN variant_color VARCHAR(50);\n\n// Update compose-v2 API:\nconst { variantColor } = body;\nawait supabaseAdmin.from('design_compositions').upsert({\n  ...,\n  variant_color: variantColor,\n  ...\n})\n\n// Update load flow:\nconst data = await load(compositionId);\nsetVariantColor(data.variant_color || 'white');\n```\n\n---\n\n### BUG #2: schema_version Mismatch (Multi-panel) ⚠️ SEVERITY: HIGH\n\n**Files:**\n- `frontend/src/components/design-studio/DesignStudioPage.tsx:204` (creates schema_version: 3)\n- `frontend/src/app/api/designs/compose-v2/route.ts:63` (always saves as schema_version: 2)\n- `frontend/src/lib/composition-renderer.ts:262-273` (expects schema_version: 2 or fallback to preview)\n\n**Problem:**\n```typescript\n// DesignStudioPage line 204\nconst result = await save({\n  fabricJson: { panels, schema_version: 3 },  // ← Client says v3\n  ...\n})\n\n// compose-v2 API line 63\nconst { error: dbError } = await supabaseAdmin\n  .from('design_compositions')\n  .upsert({\n    ...,\n    schema_version: 2,  // ← API always saves v2! BUG\n    layers: fabricJson,  // ← stores entire { panels, schema_version: 3 } here\n    ...\n  })\n```\n\nWhen reloading composition:\n```typescript\n// DesignStudioPage line 118\nif (data.schema_version === 3 && ...) {  // ← Check for v3\n  const panels = (data.layers as { panels: ... }).panels\n  // Works IF schema_version === 3 in DB\n  // But API saved it as v2! So this condition fails.\n}\n```\n\n**Impact:** Multi-panel designs (front + back) only reload front panel on re-open\n\n**Fix Required:**\n```typescript\n// compose-v2 should detect schema from payload:\nconst schemaVersion = fabricJson && 'schema_version' in fabricJson ? 3 : 2;\n\nawait supabaseAdmin.from('design_compositions').upsert({\n  ...,\n  schema_version: schemaVersion,  // ← Dynamic\n  ...\n})\n```\n\n---\n\n### BUG #3: Color Mismatch: Design ≠ Order ⚠️ SEVERITY: CRITICAL\n\n**Files:**\n- `frontend/src/components/products/ProductDetailClient.tsx:98, 267-318`\n- `frontend/src/app/api/cart/route.ts:264-408`\n- `frontend/src/app/api/checkout/create-session/route.ts:369-481`\n\n**Problem:**\n```\nWorkflow:\n1. User designs on RED garment\n2. Saves design → composition_id ABC\n3. Clicks \"Apply to Cart\" → navigates to /shop/{id}?compositionId=ABC\n4. ProductDetailClient renders with color selector\n5. DEFAULT color is BLUE (first available color from variants)\n6. User adds to cart with BLUE variant + composition_id ABC\n7. At checkout: Printify temp product uses composition design (made on red)\n             BUT order variant is blue\n8. Result: RED design printed on BLUE garment (wrong!)\n```\n\n**Evidence:**\n```typescript\n// ProductDetailClient line 107-113\nconst [selectedColor, setSelectedColor] = useState<string>(\n  initialColor && colors?.includes(initialColor)\n    ? initialColor\n    : (colors?.length === 1\n        ? colors[0]\n        : (hasColorMapping && colors && colors.length > 0 ? colors[0] : ''))\n)\n// ← Always defaults to colors[0], IGNORING composition color\n\n// handleAddToCart (line 267-318)\nawait addToCart(\n  ...,\n  { size: selectedSize || undefined, color: selectedColor || undefined },\n  ...,\n  compositionIdParam || undefined  // ← Color and composition are INDEPENDENT\n)\n```\n\n**Impact:** Orders arrive with design on wrong garment color\n\n**Fix Required:**\n1. **When loading composition**, also load its variant_color:\n```typescript\nconst compositionIdParam = searchParams.get('compositionId')\nconst [composition, setComposition] = useState(null)\n\nuseEffect(() => {\n  if (!compositionIdParam) return\n  fetch(`/api/designs/composition/${compositionIdParam}`)\n    .then(r => r.json())\n    .then(data => {\n      setComposition(data)\n      setSelectedColor(data.variant_color || colors?.[0] || '')  // ← Use composition color\n    })\n}, [compositionIdParam])\n```\n\n2. **Lock color selector** when composition is applied:\n```typescript\n<SelectTrigger disabled={!!compositionIdParam}>\n  {/* color options */}\n</SelectTrigger>\n```\n\n3. **Or: validate at checkout** that variant color matches composition color\n\n---\n\n### BUG #4: Production Export Missing for Fabric.js ⚠️ SEVERITY: HIGH\n\n**Files:**\n- `frontend/src/lib/composition-renderer.ts:243-273` (exportForProduction)\n- `frontend/src/app/api/checkout/create-session/route.ts:376-377`\n\n**Problem:**\n```typescript\n// exportForProduction line 262-273\nif (composition.schema_version === 2) {\n  // For Fabric.js compositions, the client exports the final PNG.\n  // Production export is handled client-side via canvas.toDataURL() with multiplier.\n  // If a production_url is already set (from client export), return it.\n  if (composition.production_url) {\n    return composition.production_url\n  }\n  // If no production_url yet, use the preview as fallback\n  if (composition.preview_url) {\n    return composition.preview_url  // ← FALLBACK: Uses low-res preview!\n  }\n  throw new Error(`Fabric.js composition ${compositionId} has no production or preview URL`)\n}\n```\n\n**The bug:**\n- DesignStudioPage exports 1024x1024 preview PNG only (line 199)\n- Never exports high-res production PNG\n- At checkout: exportForProduction() returns preview_url (1024x1024)\n- Printify gets low-res blurry image\n- Order prints with blurry text/images\n\n**Evidence:**\n```typescript\n// DesignStudioPage line 199-205\npreviewDataUrl: canvasRef.current.exportPNG(1),  // ← 1x multiplier = 1024x1024\nsave({\n  fabricJson: { panels, schema_version: 3 },\n  previewDataUrl,  // ← Only preview, NOT production-res\n  ...\n})\n```\n\n**Comment in code** (composition-renderer line 261-263):\n```typescript\n// For Fabric.js compositions, the client exports the final PNG.\n// Production export is handled client-side via canvas.toDataURL() with multiplier.\n```\nBut nowhere in the codebase does the client call exportPNG with production multiplier!\n\n**Impact:** Prints are blurry/low-quality\n\n**Fix Required:**\n1. **Client-side**: Export production-res PNG before apply-to-cart\n2. **Or: Server-side**: Use fabricJson to re-render at production res (expensive)\n3. **Or: Hybrid**: Client exports preview (cached), server re-renders on checkout if needed\n\n---\n\n### BUG #5: RLS Policy Bypass on GET /api/designs/composition/[id] ⚠️ SEVERITY: MEDIUM\n\n**Files:**\n- `frontend/src/app/api/designs/composition/[id]/route.ts:4-30`\n\n**Problem:**\n```typescript\nexport async function GET(\n  request: NextRequest,\n  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string } } }\n) {\n  // ...\n  const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin\n    .from('design_compositions')\n    .select(...)  // ← Uses ADMIN client, bypasses RLS!\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .single()\n\n  if (error || !data) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Composition not found' }, { status: 404 })\n  }\n  return NextResponse.json(data)  // ← Returns composition WITHOUT checking user_id!\n}\n```\n\n**Impact:**\n- Any user can fetch ANY composition by guessing the UUID\n- User A's custom design can be viewed/stolen by User B\n- Security: Information disclosure\n\n**Fix Required:**\n```typescript\nconst { user } = await createServerClient(request)\n\nif (!user) {\n  return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n}\n\nconst { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin\n  .from('design_compositions')\n  .select(...)\n  .eq('id', id)\n  .eq('user_id', user.id)  // ← ADD THIS check\n  .single()\n\nif (error || !data) {\n  return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Composition not found' }, { status: 404 })\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### BUG #6: Orphaned Compositions (Saved but Never Ordered) ⚠️ SEVERITY: MEDIUM\n\n**Files:**\n- No cleanup cron exists\n- `design_compositions` table has no TTL or retention policy\n\n**Problem:**\n- Users save drafts but never order them\n- Compositions accumulate in DB indefinitely\n- Storage bloats with preview/production images\n- No way to clean up old drafts\n\n**Impact:**\n- Database growth unbounded\n- Old designs persist forever (storage cost)\n- No user control to delete drafts\n\n**Fix Required:**\n1. **Add cron job** (`/api/cron/cleanup-old-compositions`):\n```typescript\n// Delete compositions older than 90 days with status='draft'\nconst { data: oldCompositions } = await supabaseAdmin\n  .from('design_compositions')\n  .select('id')\n  .eq('status', 'draft')\n  .lt('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString())\n\n// Delete from Supabase Storage\nfor (const comp of oldCompositions) {\n  await supabaseAdmin.storage\n    .from('designs')\n    .remove([`compositions/${comp.id}/preview.png`, `compositions/${comp.id}/production.png`])\n}\n\n// Delete DB records\nawait supabaseAdmin.from('design_compositions').delete().in('id', oldCompositions.map(c => c.id))\n```\n\n2. **Or: Add user-facing \"Delete\" button** to design list\n\n---\n\n### BUG #7: Personalization + Composition Conflict ⚠️ SEVERITY: MEDIUM\n\n**Files:**\n- `frontend/src/app/api/cart/route.ts:341-359` (cart item uniqueness check)\n- `frontend/src/components/products/ProductDetailClient.tsx:267-327`\n\n**Problem:**\n```typescript\n// cart/route.ts line 341-359\n// Check if item already exists in cart\nconst existingQuery = supabase.from('cart_items').select('*')\n  .eq('product_id', product_id)\n  .eq('variant_id', variantId)\n\nif (personalization_id) {\n  existingQuery.eq('personalization_id', personalization_id)\n} else {\n  existingQuery.is('personalization_id', null)\n}\n\nif (composition_id) {\n  existingQuery.eq('composition_id', composition_id)\n} else {\n  existingQuery.is('composition_id', null)\n}\n```\n\n**The issue:**\n- Uniqueness check: `product + variant + personalization + composition` must all match\n- BUT: User can have BOTH personalization AND composition on same item\n- Workflow:\n  1. Save custom design → composition_id ABC\n  2. Add personalization text to it\n  3. Add both to cart\n  4. Later: Add personalization to same product with different text\n  5. Same composition_id ABC + different personalization\n  6. Both should be in cart as separate items ✓ (works correctly)\n\n**Actually works as intended** — no bug here. ✓\n\n---\n\n### BUG #8: Multi-panel Load Only Loads Front Panel ⚠️ SEVERITY: MEDIUM\n\n**Files:**\n- `frontend/src/components/design-studio/DesignStudioPage.tsx:110-138`\n\n**Problem:**\n```typescript\nuseEffect(() => {\n  if (!initialCompositionId) return\n\n  async function loadComposition() {\n    const data = await load(initialCompositionId!)\n    \n    if (data.schema_version === 3 && ...) {\n      const panels = (data.layers as { panels: ... }).panels\n      for (const [panel, state] of Object.entries(panels)) {\n        setPanelState(panel, { fabricJson: state.fabricJson, ... })\n      }\n      // Load front panel\n      if (panels.front && canvasRef.current) {\n        await canvasRef.current.loadFromJSON(panels.front.fabricJson)  // ← Only loads front\n      }\n    } else if (data.schema_version === 2 && data.layers) {\n      if (canvasRef.current) {\n        await canvasRef.current.loadFromJSON(data.layers)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  \n  const timer = setTimeout(loadComposition, 500)\n  return () => clearTimeout(timer)\n}, [initialCompositionId, load, setPanelState])\n```\n\n**The issue:**\n- All panels are stored in `data.layers.panels`\n- But only `panels.front` is loaded into canvas (line 125)\n- Other panels (back, left sleeve, etc.) are loaded into panelStates but user never sees them\n- When user switches to back panel, they see the saved back design ✓ (works)\n\n**Actually works correctly** — no bug here. ✓\n\n---\n\n### BUG #9: Color Change After Save Doesn't Mark Dirty ⚠️ SEVERITY: LOW\n\n**Files:**\n- `frontend/src/components/design-studio/DesignStudioPage.tsx:156-167`\n\n**Problem:**\n```typescript\nconst handleColorChange = useCallback((color: string) => {\n  // Serialize current panel before color change\n  if (canvasRef.current) {\n    const currentJson = canvasRef.current.exportJSON()\n    setPanelState(activePanel, {\n      fabricJson: currentJson,\n      isDirty: isDirty,\n    })\n  }\n  setVariantColor(color)  // ← Color changes but isDirty not set!\n}, [activePanel, isDirty, setPanelState, setVariantColor])\n```\n\n**Issue:**\n- User saves design (isDirty = false)\n- User changes garment color\n- isDirty stays false\n- If user clicks \"Apply to Cart\" without re-saving, old composition is used\n- Color mismatch BUG #3 results\n\n**Example:**\n```\n1. Design on red, save → composition ABC\n2. Change color to blue\n3. Click \"Apply to Cart\" → still uses ABC (red composition) but adds blue variant\n4. Cart has: composition ABC (red design) + blue variant\n```\n\n**Fix:**\n```typescript\nconst handleColorChange = useCallback((color: string) => {\n  if (canvasRef.current) {\n    const currentJson = canvasRef.current.exportJSON()\n    setPanelState(activePanel, {\n      fabricJson: currentJson,\n      isDirty: isDirty,\n    })\n  }\n  setVariantColor(color)\n  setDirty(true)  // ← ADD THIS\n}, [...])\n```\n\n---\n\n## SUMMARY TABLE\n\n| # | Bug | Severity | Impact | Status |\n|---|-----|----------|--------|--------|\n| 1 | variantColor not persisted | CRITICAL | Wrong color on reopened front | MEDIUM | ✓ Works correctly | No issue |\n| 9 | Color change doesn't mark dirty | LOW | Edge case in workflow | Minor fix |\n\n---\n\n## RECOMMENDED PRIORITY\n\n**IMMEDIATE (Block production
